---
title: "Orders"
description: "Orders represent work to be planned, dispatched, tracked, and completed. Use order endpoints to create delivery or service jobs, assign resources, manage status, capture proof of delivery, and record activity."
---
<ResourceHeader
  title="Orders"
  description="Orders represent work to be planned, dispatched, tracked, and completed. Use order endpoints to create delivery or service jobs, assign resources, manage status, capture proof of delivery, and record activity."
  endpoints={[
    { title: "The Order object", id: "the-order-object" },
    { title: "Create an Order", id: "create-an-order", method: "POST", path: "/v1/orders" },
    { title: "Query Orders", id: "query-orders", method: "GET", path: "/v1/orders" },
    { title: "Schedule an Order", id: "schedule-an-order", method: "PATCH", path: "/v1/orders/:id/schedule" },
    { title: "Dispatch an Order", id: "dispatch-an-order", method: "PATCH", path: "/v1/orders/:id/dispatch" },
    { title: "Start an Order", id: "start-an-order", method: "POST", path: "/v1/orders/:id/start" },
    { title: "Update Order Activity", id: "update-order-activity", method: "POST", path: "/v1/orders/:id/update-activity" },
    { title: "Get Order Next Activity", id: "get-order-next-activity", method: "GET", path: "/v1/orders/:id/next-activity" },
    { title: "Set Order Destination", id: "set-order-destination", method: "PATCH", path: "/v1/orders/:id/set-destination/:placeId" },
    { title: "Capture Signature for Order", id: "capture-signature-for-order", method: "POST", path: "/v1/orders/:id/capture-signature/:subject-id" },
    { title: "Capture QR Code for Order", id: "capture-qr-code-for-order", method: "POST", path: "/v1/orders/:id/capture-qr/:subject-id" },
    { title: "Retrieve an Order", id: "retrieve-an-order", method: "GET", path: "/v1/orders" },
    { title: "Update an Order", id: "update-an-order", method: "PUT", path: "/v1/orders/:id" },
    { title: "Cancel an Order", id: "cancel-an-order", method: "DELETE", path: "/v1/orders/:id/cancel" },
    { title: "Delete an Order", id: "delete-an-order", method: "DELETE", path: "/v1/orders/:id" },
    { title: "Get Order Distance and Time", id: "get-order-distance-and-time", method: "GET", path: "/v1/orders/:id/distance-and-time" },
    { title: "Complete an Order", id: "complete-an-order", method: "POST", path: "/v1/orders/:id/complete" },
    { title: "Get Order Tracker", id: "get-order-tracker", method: "GET", path: "/v1/orders/:id/tracker" },
    { title: "Get Order ETA", id: "get-order-eta", method: "GET", path: "/v1/orders/:id/eta" },
    { title: "List Order Comments", id: "list-order-comments", method: "GET", path: "/v1/orders/:id/comments" },
    { title: "Capture Photo for Order", id: "capture-photo-for-order", method: "POST", path: "/v1/orders/:id/capture-photo/:subjectId" },
    { title: "List Order Proofs", id: "list-order-proofs", method: "GET", path: "/v1/orders/:id/proofs/:subjectId" },
    { title: "Get Editable Entity Fields", id: "get-editable-entity-fields", method: "GET", path: "/v1/orders/:id/editable-entity-fields" },
  ]}
/>
<ResourceObject
  name="Order"
  id="the-order-object"
  description="An order represents a FleetOps delivery or service workflow. It links a payload, optional customer and facilitator, assigned driver and vehicle resources, tracking data, proof-of-delivery settings, and lifecycle timestamps."

  "id": "order_7YqM3KpL2n",
  "internal_id": "ORD-1001",
  "customer": {
    "id": "contact_6zV1KqN9pR",
    "name": "Acme Receiving"
  },
  "payload": {
    "id": "payload_x9Z2Lm8QpA",
    "pickup": {
      "id": "place_1A2b3C4d5E",
      "name": "Warehouse 7"
    },
    "dropoff": {
      "id": "place_9Z8y7X6w5V",
      "name": "Customer Dock"
    },
    "waypoints": [],
    "entities": []
  },
  "facilitator": null,
  "driver_assigned": null,
  "vehicle_assigned": null,
  "tracking_number": {
    "id": "tracking_3Nf8Qw2YxM",
    "tracking_number": "FB-1001"
  },
  "purchase_rate": null,
  "notes": "Deliver through receiving bay.",
  "type": "transport",
  "status": "created",
  "pod_method": null,
  "pod_required": false,
  "dispatched": false,
  "started": false,
  "adhoc": false,
  "adhoc_distance": 0,
  "distance": 0,
  "time": 0,
  "meta": {},
  "dispatched_at": null,
  "started_at": null,
  "scheduled_at": null,
  "updated_at": "2026-05-07T08:30:00.000000Z",
  "created_at": "2026-05-07T08:30:00.000000Z"
}`}
  exampleHtml={`<pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light github-dark" style="--shiki-light:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;--shiki-light-bg:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e" tabindex="0"><code><span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">{</span></span>

>
  <ParamTable title="Attributes">
    <Param name="id" type="string">
      Public order identifier.
    </Param>
    <Param name="internal_id" type="string">
      Optional internal identifier for the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="order_config" type="string">
      Public identifier of the order configuration used by the order flow.
    </Param>
    <Param name="customer" type="object">
      Customer contact or vendor resource loaded with the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="payload" type="object">
      Payload resource containing the pickup, dropoff, return stop, waypoints, and entities for the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="facilitator" type="object">
      Contact, vendor, or integrated vendor facilitating the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="driver_assigned" type="object">
      Driver assigned to the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="vehicle_assigned" type="object">
      Vehicle assigned to the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="tracking_number" type="object">
      Tracking number resource generated for the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="tracking_statuses" type="array of objects">
      Tracking statuses loaded with the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="files" type="array of objects">
      Files attached to the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="purchase_rate" type="object">
      Purchase rate attached to the order, when present.
    </Param>
    <Param name="notes" type="string">
      Free-form notes attached to the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="type" type="string">
      Order type resolved from the order configuration.
    </Param>
    <Param name="status" type="string">
      Current order lifecycle status.
    </Param>
    <Param name="pod_method" type="string">
      Proof-of-delivery method required for the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="pod_required" type="boolean">
      Whether proof of delivery is required.
    </Param>
    <Param name="dispatched" type="boolean">
      Whether the order has been dispatched.
    </Param>
    <Param name="started" type="boolean">
      Whether the order has started.
    </Param>
    <Param name="adhoc" type="boolean">
      Whether the order is ad hoc.
    </Param>
    <Param name="adhoc_distance" type="integer">
      Ad hoc dispatch distance in meters.
    </Param>
    <Param name="distance" type="integer">
      Preliminary route distance in meters.
    </Param>
    <Param name="time" type="integer">
      Preliminary route duration in seconds.
    </Param>
    <Param name="tracker_data" type="object">
      Tracker data returned when requested with `with_tracker_data`.
    </Param>
    <Param name="eta" type="object">
      Estimated arrival data returned when requested with `with_eta`.
    </Param>
    <Param name="meta" type="object">
      Additional order metadata.
    </Param>
    <Param name="dispatched_at" type="timestamp">
      Timestamp when the order was dispatched.
    </Param>
    <Param name="started_at" type="timestamp">
      Timestamp when the order was started.
    </Param>
    <Param name="scheduled_at" type="timestamp">
      Timestamp when the order is scheduled.
    </Param>
    <Param name="updated_at" type="timestamp">
      Timestamp when the order was last updated.
    </Param>
    <Param name="created_at" type="timestamp">
      Timestamp when the order was created.
    </Param>
  </ParamTable>

</ResourceObject>
<Endpoint method="POST" path="/v1/orders" title="Create an Order" id="create-an-order">
  Creates a new order for the current company. Provide an existing payload ID, an inline payload, a pickup/dropoff pair, or at least two waypoints; LogisBase creates or attaches the payload before creating the order.
  
  You can assign a driver, vehicle, facilitator, customer, service quote, or schedule at creation time. When `dispatch` is true, LogisBase dispatches the order after it is created unless the order belongs to an integrated vendor flow.

  <ParamTable title="Body parameters">
    <Param name="payload" type="object">
      Existing payload ID or an inline payload object. If omitted, provide `pickup` and `dropoff`, or provide at least two `waypoints`.
      <Param name="pickup" type="object">
        Pickup place data or place identifier for the payload.
      </Param>
      <Param name="dropoff" type="object">
        Dropoff place data or place identifier for the payload.
      </Param>
      <Param name="return" type="object">
        Optional return place data or place identifier for the payload.
      </Param>
      <Param name="waypoints" type="array of objects">
        Ordered route stops. Required when the payload does not include both `pickup` and `dropoff`.
      </Param>
      <Param name="entities" type="array of objects">
        Entities, packages, or items attached to the payload.
      </Param>
    </Param>
    <Param name="pickup" type="object">
      Pickup place data or address used to create the order payload when `payload` is not supplied.
    </Param>
    <Param name="dropoff" type="object">
      Dropoff place data or address used to create the order payload when `payload` is not supplied.
    </Param>
    <Param name="return" type="object">
      Optional return place data or address used when creating the order payload.
    </Param>
    <Param name="waypoints" type="array of objects">
      Ordered route stops used when creating the order payload without a pickup/dropoff pair. At least two waypoints are required when no pickup/dropoff pair is provided.
    </Param>
    <Param name="entities" type="array of objects">
      Entities, packages, or items attached to the generated payload.
    </Param>
    <Param name="type" type="string">
      Order configuration key. LogisBase resolves this with `order_config` and stores the resolved key as the order type.
    </Param>
    <Param name="order_config" type="string">
      Order configuration identifier used to resolve the order flow.
    </Param>
    <Param name="service_quote" type="string">
      Service quote ID to purchase and attach to the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="purchase_rate" type="string">
      Purchase rate ID to attach to the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="driver" type="string">
      Driver ID to assign to the order. LogisBase also assigns the driver's vehicle when one is available.
    </Param>
    <Param name="vehicle" type="string">
      Vehicle ID to assign to the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="facilitator" type="string">
      Contact, vendor, or integrated vendor ID responsible for facilitating the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="customer" type="object">
      Existing contact/vendor ID, or customer details used to find or create a customer contact.
      <Param name="internal_id" type="string">
        Optional internal customer identifier.
      </Param>
      <Param name="name" type="string">
        Customer display name.
      </Param>
      <Param name="title" type="string">
        Customer title.
      </Param>
      <Param name="email" type="string">
        Customer email address used when finding or creating the customer contact.
      </Param>
      <Param name="phone" type="string">
        Customer phone number.
      </Param>
      <Param name="meta" type="object">
        Additional customer metadata.
      </Param>
    </Param>
    <Param name="dispatch" type="boolean">
      When `true`, LogisBase dispatches the order after it is created. Dispatch is skipped for integrated vendor orders.
    </Param>
    <Param name="adhoc" type="boolean">
      Marks the order as ad hoc.
    </Param>
    <Param name="adhoc_distance" type="number">
      Ad hoc search distance for dispatching nearby drivers.
    </Param>
    <Param name="pod_required" type="boolean">
      Whether proof of delivery is required to complete the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="pod_method" type="string">
      Proof of delivery method. Required when `pod_required` is true.
    </Param>
    <Param name="scheduled_at" type="timestamp">
      Date and time when the order should be scheduled.
    </Param>
    <Param name="status" type="string">
      Initial order status. Defaults to `created` when omitted.
    </Param>
    <Param name="internal_id" type="string">
      Optional internal identifier for the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="meta" type="object">
      Additional order metadata.
    </Param>
    <Param name="notes" type="string">
      Free-form notes attached to the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="time_window_start" type="timestamp">
      Earliest time the order should be serviced by the orchestrator.
    </Param>
    <Param name="time_window_end" type="timestamp">
      Latest time the order should be serviced by the orchestrator.
    </Param>
    <Param name="required_skills" type="array of strings">
      Skills required for orchestrator assignment.
    </Param>
    <Param name="orchestrator_priority" type="integer" default="50">
      Orchestrator priority for assignment. LogisBase defaults invalid or omitted values to `50`.
    </Param>
  </ParamTable>

        php: { code: `<?php
$client = new \\GuzzleHttp\\Client();

$response = $client->post('https://api.logisbase.com/v1/orders', [
    'headers' => ['Authorization' => 'Bearer flb_live_…'],
    'json' => [
    'pickup' => 'Singapore 018971',
    'dropoff' => '321 Orchard Rd, Singapore',
    'waypoints' => [
        '10 Bayfront Avenue, Singapore 018956',
        '18 Marina Gardens Drive, Singapore 018953',
        '80 Mandai Lake Rd, Singapore 729826',
        '1 Beach Road, Singapore 189673',
    ],
    'dispatch' => false,
    'driver' => '{{driver_id}}',
    'facilitator' => '{{vendor_id}}',
    'customer' => '{{contact_id}}',
    'meta' => [
        'Warehouse' => 'WAREHOUSE-123',
    ],
    'notes' => 'Order notes',
]
]);
$data = json_decode($response->getBody(), true);`, html: `<pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light github-dark" style="--shiki-light:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;--shiki-light-bg:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e" tabindex="0"><code><span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&#x3C;?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">php</span></span>

  ]

        php: { code: `<?php
$client = new \\GuzzleHttp\\Client();

$response = $client->post('https://api.logisbase.com/v1/orders', [
    'headers' => ['Authorization' => 'Bearer flb_live_…'],
    'json' => [
    'payload' => [
        'pickup' => 'Singapore 018971',
        'dropoff' => '321 Orchard Rd, Singapore',
        'entities' => [
            [
                'name' => 'UltraHD 4K Smart TV',
                'description' => '65-inch high-definition smart TV with vibrant colors and a sleek design.',
                'currency' => 'USD',
                'price' => 1200,
            ],
            [
                'name' => 'Bluetooth Wireless Headphones',
                'description' => 'Noise-cancelling, over-ear headphones with long-lasting battery life.',
                'currency' => 'USD',
                'price' => 250,
            ],
            [
                'name' => 'Smart Fitness Watch',
                'description' => 'Water-resistant fitness watch with heart rate monitor and GPS tracking.',
                'currency' => 'USD',
                'price' => 199.99,
            ],
        ],
    ],
    'meta' => [
        'Warehouse' => 'WAREHOUSE-123',
    ],
    'notes' => 'Order notes',
]
]);
$data = json_decode($response->getBody(), true);`, html: `<pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light github-dark" style="--shiki-light:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;--shiki-light-bg:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e" tabindex="0"><code><span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&#x3C;?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">php</span></span>

        { title: "Create an Order using Waypoints and Entity Destinations", samples: {
        curl: { code: `curl -X POST https://api.logisbase.com/v1/orders \\
  -H "Authorization: Bearer flb_live_…" \\
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \\
  -H "Accept: application/json" \\
  -d '{
    "payload": {
        "waypoints": [
            "Singapore 018971",
            "321 Orchard Rd, Singapore"
        ],
        "entities": [
            {
                "destination": 0,
                "name": "UltraHD 4K Smart TV",
                "description": "65-inch high-definition smart TV with vibrant colors and a sleek design.",
                "currency": "USD",
                "price": 1200.00
            },
            {
                "destination": 0,
                "name": "Bluetooth Wireless Headphones",
                "description": "Noise-cancelling, over-ear headphones with long-lasting battery life.",
                "currency": "USD",
                "price": 250.00
            },
            {
                "destination": 1,
                "name": "Smart Fitness Watch",
                "description": "Water-resistant fitness watch with heart rate monitor and GPS tracking.",
                "currency": "USD",
                "price": 199.99
            }
        ]
    },
    "meta": {
        "Warehouse": "WAREHOUSE-123"
    },
    "notes": "Order notes"
}'`, html: `<pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light github-dark" style="--shiki-light:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;--shiki-light-bg:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e" tabindex="0"><code><span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">curl</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> -X</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> POST</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> https://api.logisbase.com/v1/orders</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> \\</span></span>

        js: { code: `const response = await fetch('https://api.logisbase.com/v1/orders', {
  method: 'POST',
  headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer flb_live_…', 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
  body: JSON.stringify({
  "payload": {
    "waypoints": [
      "Singapore 018971",
      "321 Orchard Rd, Singapore"
    ],
    "entities": [
      {
        "destination": 0,
        "name": "UltraHD 4K Smart TV",
        "description": "65-inch high-definition smart TV with vibrant colors and a sleek design.",
        "currency": "USD",
        "price": 1200
      },
      {
        "destination": 0,
        "name": "Bluetooth Wireless Headphones",
        "description": "Noise-cancelling, over-ear headphones with long-lasting battery life.",
        "currency": "USD",
        "price": 250

      {
        "destination": 1,

      }
    ]
  },
  "meta": {
    "Warehouse": "WAREHOUSE-123"
  },
  "notes": "Order notes"
})
});

        php: { code: `<?php
$client = new \\GuzzleHttp\\Client();

$response = $client->post('https://api.logisbase.com/v1/orders', [
    'headers' => ['Authorization' => 'Bearer flb_live_…'],
    'json' => [
    'payload' => [
        'waypoints' => [
            'Singapore 018971',
            '321 Orchard Rd, Singapore',
        ],
        'entities' => [
            [
                'destination' => 0,
                'name' => 'UltraHD 4K Smart TV',
                'description' => '65-inch high-definition smart TV with vibrant colors and a sleek design.',
                'currency' => 'USD',
                'price' => 1200,
            ],
            [
                'destination' => 0,
                'name' => 'Bluetooth Wireless Headphones',
                'description' => 'Noise-cancelling, over-ear headphones with long-lasting battery life.',
                'currency' => 'USD',
                'price' => 250,
            ],
            [
                'destination' => 1,
                'name' => 'Smart Fitness Watch',
                'description' => 'Water-resistant fitness watch with heart rate monitor and GPS tracking.',
                'currency' => 'USD',
                'price' => 199.99,
            ],
        ],
    ],
    'meta' => [
        'Warehouse' => 'WAREHOUSE-123',
    ],
    'notes' => 'Order notes',
]
]);
$data = json_decode($response->getBody(), true);`, html: `<pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light github-dark" style="--shiki-light:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;--shiki-light-bg:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e" tabindex="0"><code><span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&#x3C;?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">php</span></span>

response = requests.post(

        'waypoints': [

                'name': 'UltraHD 4K Smart TV',

                'currency': 'USD',
                'price': 1200,
            },

                'name': 'Bluetooth Wireless Headphones',

                'description': 'Water-resistant fitness watch with heart rate monitor and GPS tracking.',
                'currency': 'USD',
                'price': 199.99,
            },
        ],
    },
    'meta': {
        'Warehouse': 'WAREHOUSE-123',
    },
    'notes': 'Order notes',
},
)

        { title: "Create an Order using Waypoints and Entities with Photos", samples: {
        curl: { code: `curl -X POST https://api.logisbase.com/v1/orders \\
  -H "Authorization: Bearer flb_live_…" \\
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \\
  -H "Accept: application/json" \\
  -d '{
    "payload": {
        "waypoints": [
            "Singapore 018971",
            "321 Orchard Rd, Singapore"
        ],
        "entities": [
            {
                "destination": 0,
                "name": "UltraHD 4K Smart TV",
                "description": "65-inch high-definition smart TV with vibrant colors and a sleek design.",
                "currency": "USD",
                "price": 1200.00,
                "photo": "https://cdn.thewirecutter.com/wp-content/media/2025/07/BEST-BUDGET-4K-TV-2048px-3185-2x1-1.jpg?width=1024&quality=75&crop=2:1&auto=webp"
            },
            {
                "destination": 0,
                "name": "Bluetooth Wireless Headphones",
                "description": "Noise-cancelling, over-ear headphones with long-lasting battery life.",
                "currency": "USD",
                "price": 250.00,
                "photo": "https://cdn.thewirecutter.com/wp-content/media/2025/07/BEST-BUDGET-4K-TV-2048px-3185-2x1-1.jpg?width=1024&quality=75&crop=2:1&auto=webp"
            },
            {
                "destination": 1,
                "name": "Smart Fitness Watch",

        php: { code: `<?php
$client = new \\GuzzleHttp\\Client();

$response = $client->post('https://api.logisbase.com/v1/orders', [
    'headers' => ['Authorization' => 'Bearer flb_live_…'],
    'json' => [
    'payload' => [
        'waypoints' => [
            'Singapore 018971',
            '321 Orchard Rd, Singapore',
        ],
        'entities' => [
            [
                'destination' => 0,
                'name' => 'UltraHD 4K Smart TV',
                'description' => '65-inch high-definition smart TV with vibrant colors and a sleek design.',

        python: { code: `import requests

response = requests.post(
    'https://api.logisbase.com/v1/orders',
    headers={'Authorization': 'Bearer flb_live_…'},
    json={
    'payload': {
        'waypoints': [
            'Singapore 018971',
            '321 Orchard Rd, Singapore',
        ],
        'entities': [
            {
                'destination': 0,
                'name': 'UltraHD 4K Smart TV',
                'description': '65-inch high-definition smart TV with vibrant colors and a sleek design.',
                'currency': 'USD',
                'price': 1200,
                'photo': 'https://cdn.thewirecutter.com/wp-content/media/2025/07/BEST-BUDGET-4K-TV-2048px-3185-2x1-1.jpg?width=1024&quality=75&crop=2:1&auto=webp',
            },
            {
                'destination': 0,
                'name': 'Bluetooth Wireless Headphones',
                'description': 'Noise-cancelling, over-ear headphones with long-lasting battery life.',
                'currency': 'USD',
                'price': 250,
                'photo': 'https://cdn.thewirecutter.com/wp-content/media/2025/07/BEST-BUDGET-4K-TV-2048px-3185-2x1-1.jpg?width=1024&quality=75&crop=2:1&auto=webp',
            },
            {
                'destination': 1,
                'name': 'Smart Fitness Watch',
                'description': 'Water-resistant fitness watch with heart rate monitor and GPS tracking.',
                'currency': 'USD',
                'price': 199.99,
                'photo': 'https://cdn.thewirecutter.com/wp-content/media/2025/07/BEST-BUDGET-4K-TV-2048px-3185-2x1-1.jpg?width=1024&quality=75&crop=2:1&auto=webp',
            },
        ],
    },
    'meta': {
        'Warehouse': 'WAREHOUSE-123',
    },
    'notes': 'Order notes',
},
)
data = response.json()`, html: `<pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light github-dark" style="--shiki-light:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;--shiki-light-bg:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e" tabindex="0"><code><span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> requests</span></span>

<Endpoint method="GET" path="/v1/orders" title="Query Orders" id="query-orders">
  Returns orders for the current company. Use filters such as `status`, `payload`, `customer`, `facilitator`, or `nearby` to narrow the result set.

  <ParamTable title="Query parameters">
    <Param name="limit" type="integer">
      Maximum number of orders to return.
    </Param>
    <Param name="offset" type="integer">
      Number of orders to skip before returning results.
    </Param>
    <Param name="sort" type="string">
      Sort expression for the order query.
    </Param>
    <Param name="status" type="string">
      Filters orders by lifecycle status.
    </Param>
    <Param name="payload" type="string">
      Filters orders by payload ID.
    </Param>
    <Param name="pickup" type="string">
      Filters orders by pickup place ID.
    </Param>
    <Param name="dropoff" type="string">
      Filters orders by dropoff place ID.
    </Param>
    <Param name="return" type="string">
      Filters orders by return place ID.
    </Param>
    <Param name="facilitator" type="string">
      Filters orders by facilitator public or internal ID.
    </Param>
    <Param name="customer" type="string">
      Filters orders by customer public or internal ID.
    </Param>
    <Param name="entity" type="string">
      Filters orders containing the specified entity public or internal ID.
    </Param>
    <Param name="entity_status" type="string">
      Filters orders by the tracking status code of their payload entities.
    </Param>
    <Param name="on" type="date">
      Filters orders created or scheduled on the specified date.
    </Param>
    <Param name="pod_required" type="boolean">
      When true, returns only orders requiring proof of delivery.
    </Param>
    <Param name="dispatched" type="boolean">
      When true, returns only dispatched orders.
    </Param>
    <Param name="nearby" type="string">
      Filters nearby orders using coordinates, a driver ID, or an address string.
    </Param>
  </ParamTable>

  <HttpExample>
    <HttpRequest
      method="GET"
      path="/v1/orders"
      samples={{
        curl: { code: `curl https://api.logisbase.com/v1/orders?limit=25&offset=0&sort=created_at&status=created \\

<Endpoint method="PATCH" path="/v1/orders/:id/schedule" title="Schedule an Order" id="schedule-an-order">
  Schedules an order for a specific date and optional time. LogisBase parses the schedule using the supplied timezone or the company timezone.

  <ParamTable title="Body parameters">
    <Param name="date" type="date" required>
      Schedule date in `YYYY-MM-DD` format.
    </Param>
    <Param name="time" type="string">
      Optional time to combine with `date`.
    </Param>
    <Param name="timezone" type="string">
      Timezone used when parsing the schedule date and time. Defaults to the company timezone.
    </Param>
  </ParamTable>

<Endpoint method="PATCH" path="/v1/orders/:id/dispatch" title="Dispatch an Order" id="dispatch-an-order">

<Endpoint method="POST" path="/v1/orders/:id/start" title="Start an Order" id="start-an-order">
  Starts an order and transitions it into active execution. Use this when a driver or dispatcher begins fulfillment.

  <ParamTable title="Body parameters">
    <Param name="skip_dispatch" type="boolean">
      Starts the order even if it has not been dispatched.
    </Param>
    <Param name="skipDispatch" type="boolean">
      Camel-case alias for `skip_dispatch`.
    </Param>
    <Param name="assign" type="string">
      Driver ID to assign before starting an ad hoc order.
    </Param>
  </ParamTable>

  <HttpExample>
    <HttpRequest
      method="POST"
      path="/v1/orders/:id/start"
      samples={{
        curl: { code: `curl -X POST https://api.logisbase.com/v1/orders/:id/start \\
  -H "Authorization: Bearer flb_live_…" \\
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \\
  -d '{
    "skip_dispatch": false
}'`, html: `<pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light github-dark" style="--shiki-light:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;--shiki-light-bg:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e" tabindex="0"><code><span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">curl</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> -X</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> POST</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> https://api.logisbase.com/v1/orders/:id/start</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> \\</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">  -H</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> "Authorization: Bearer flb_live_…"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> \\</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">  -d</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> '{</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">}'</span></span></code></pre>` },
        js: { code: `const response = await fetch('https://api.logisbase.com/v1/orders/:id/start', {
  method: 'POST',

  body: JSON.stringify({
  "skip_dispatch": false
})
});

<Endpoint method="POST" path="/v1/orders/:id/update-activity" title="Update Order Activity" id="update-order-activity">
  Updates the current activity state for an order. The response returns the order with the latest workflow activity applied.

  <ParamTable title="Body parameters">
    <Param name="activity" type="object">
      Activity payload to apply to the order. LogisBase resolves it against the order configuration flow.
    </Param>
    <Param name="proof" type="object">
      Optional proof data to attach when the activity updates an entity or waypoint.
    </Param>
    <Param name="skip_dispatch" type="boolean">
      Uses the first started activity instead of dispatching when the submitted activity is `dispatched`.
    </Param>
    <Param name="skipDispatch" type="boolean">
      Camel-case alias for `skip_dispatch`.
    </Param>
  </ParamTable>

<Endpoint method="POST" path="/v1/orders/:id/capture-signature/:subject-id" title="Capture Signature for Order" id="capture-signature-for-order">
  Captures a signature proof for an order or order subject. Use this when a workflow requires signed proof of delivery or pickup.

  <ParamTable title="Body parameters">
    <Param name="signature" type="string" required>
      Base64 PNG signature data. LogisBase stores the image and links it to the proof record.
    </Param>
    <Param name="remarks" type="string" default="Verified by Signature">
      Remarks stored on the proof record.
    </Param>
    <Param name="data" type="object">
      Structured proof metadata stored with the signature proof.
    </Param>
    <Param name="disk" type="string">
      Filesystem disk used to store the signature image. Defaults to the configured filesystem disk.
    </Param>
    <Param name="bucket" type="string">
      Storage bucket used for the generated signature file.
    </Param>
  </ParamTable>

  <HttpExample>
    <HttpRequest
      method="POST"
      path="/v1/orders/:id/capture-signature/:subject-id"
      samples={{
        curl: { code: `curl -X POST https://api.logisbase.com/v1/orders/:id/capture-signature/:subject-id \\
  -H "Authorization: Bearer flb_live_…" \\
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \\
  -d '{
    "signature": "",
    "data": []
}'`, html: `<pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light github-dark" style="--shiki-light:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;--shiki-light-bg:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e" tabindex="0"><code><span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">curl</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> -X</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> POST</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> https://api.logisbase.com/v1/orders/:id/capture-signature/:subject-id</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> \\</span></span>

<Endpoint method="POST" path="/v1/orders/:id/capture-qr/:subject-id" title="Capture QR Code for Order" id="capture-qr-code-for-order">
  Captures a QR code proof for an order or order subject. The response includes the updated proof data associated with the order.

  <ParamTable title="Body parameters">
    <Param name="code" type="string" required>
      QR code value to verify. The code must match the resolved subject UUID.
    </Param>
    <Param name="data" type="object">
      Structured proof metadata stored with the QR proof.
    </Param>
    <Param name="raw_data" type="object">
      Raw QR payload stored on the proof record.
    </Param>
  </ParamTable>

<Endpoint method="PUT" path="/v1/orders/:id" title="Update an Order" id="update-an-order">
  Updates an order and returns the updated order resource. You can update order metadata, notes, status, assignment fields, scheduling fields, proof-of-delivery settings, and payload details.

  <ParamTable title="Body parameters">
    <Param name="payload" type="object">
      Existing payload ID or inline payload data used to replace or update the order payload.
    </Param>
    <Param name="pickup" type="object">
      Replacement pickup place data or address for the order payload.
    </Param>
    <Param name="dropoff" type="object">
      Replacement dropoff place data or address for the order payload.
    </Param>
    <Param name="return" type="object">
      Replacement return place data or address for the order payload.
    </Param>
    <Param name="waypoints" type="array of objects">
      Replacement waypoint list. When sent empty with route endpoint fields, existing waypoints are removed.
    </Param>
    <Param name="entities" type="array of objects">
      Replacement entities for the order payload.
    </Param>
    <Param name="service_quote" type="string">
      Service quote ID to purchase and attach to the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="driver" type="string">
      Driver ID to assign to the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="vehicle" type="string">
      Vehicle ID to assign to the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="facilitator" type="string">
      Contact or vendor ID responsible for facilitating the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="customer" type="string">
      Contact or vendor ID to assign as the order customer.
    </Param>
    <Param name="dispatch" type="boolean">
      When `true`, dispatches the order after applying the update.
    </Param>
    <Param name="adhoc" type="boolean">
      Marks the order as ad hoc.
    </Param>
    <Param name="adhoc_distance" type="number">
      Ad hoc search distance for dispatching nearby drivers.
    </Param>
    <Param name="pod_required" type="boolean">
      Whether proof of delivery is required to complete the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="pod_method" type="string">
      Proof of delivery method. Required when `pod_required` is true.
    </Param>
    <Param name="scheduled_at" type="timestamp">
      Date and time when the order should be scheduled.
    </Param>
    <Param name="type" type="string">
      Order type or order configuration key.
    </Param>
    <Param name="status" type="string">
      Order lifecycle status to set.
    </Param>
    <Param name="internal_id" type="string">
      Optional internal identifier for the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="meta" type="object">
      Additional order metadata.
    </Param>
    <Param name="notes" type="string">
      Free-form notes attached to the order.
    </Param>
    <Param name="time_window_start" type="timestamp">
      Earliest time the order should be serviced by the orchestrator.
    </Param>
    <Param name="time_window_end" type="timestamp">
      Latest time the order should be serviced by the orchestrator.
    </Param>
    <Param name="required_skills" type="array of strings">
      Skills required for orchestrator assignment.
    </Param>
    <Param name="orchestrator_priority" type="integer" default="50">
      Orchestrator priority for assignment. LogisBase defaults explicitly empty values to `50`.
    </Param>
  </ParamTable>

  <HttpExample>
    <HttpRequest
      method="PUT"
      path="/v1/orders/:id"
      samples={{
        curl: { code: `curl -X PUT https://api.logisbase.com/v1/orders/:id \\
  -H "Authorization: Bearer flb_live_…" \\
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \\
  -d '{
  "service_quote": "quote_hZ3C21KVkx"
}'`, html: `<pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light github-dark" style="--shiki-light:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;--shiki-light-bg:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e" tabindex="0"><code><span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">curl</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> -X</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> PUT</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> https://api.logisbase.com/v1/orders/:id</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> \\</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">  -H</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> "Authorization: Bearer flb_live_…"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> \\</span></span>

<Endpoint method="POST" path="/v1/orders/:id/capture-photo/:subjectId" title="Capture Photo for Order" id="capture-photo-for-order">
  Captures proof photos for an order or order subject.

  <ParamTable title="Body parameters">
    <Param name="photos" type="array of strings" required>
      One or more uploaded images or Base64 image strings. Each item must be an image no larger than 10 MB.
    </Param>
    <Param name="remarks" type="string" default="Verified by Photo">
      Remarks stored on each proof record.
    </Param>
    <Param name="data" type="object">
      Structured proof metadata stored with each photo proof.
    </Param>
    <Param name="disk" type="string">
      Filesystem disk used to store proof photos. Defaults to the configured filesystem disk.
    </Param>
  </ParamTable>

  <HttpExample>
    <HttpRequest
      method="POST"
      path="/v1/orders/:id/capture-photo/:subjectId"
      samples={{

  -d '{
  "photos": [
    "{{proof_photo_base64}}"
  ],
  "remarks": "Verified by Photo",
  "data": {}
}'`, html: `<pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light github-dark" style="--shiki-light:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8;--shiki-light-bg:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e" tabindex="0"><code><span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">curl</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> -X</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> POST</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF"> https://api.logisbase.com/v1/orders/:id/capture-photo/:subjectId</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> \\</span></span>
